Argument - a git range, default the changes not yet committed. ([xsko/elasticore-agent-kit](https://github.com/xsko/elasticore-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/opencode/commands/elasticore-change-report.md) · ★2)
- **elasticore-generate**: Regenerate an ElastiCORE project from its DSL, compile it, and report what the generator said - written, skipped and failed files, DSL validation warnings, hand-edited generated files - with the DSL fix for each problem. Argument - a Gradle task, default compileJava. ([xsko/elasticore-agent-kit](https://github.com/xsko/elasticore-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/opencode/commands/elasticore-generate.md) · ★2)
- **elasticore-migrate**: Move an ElastiCORE project that 2.x generated (or that keeps output = 'v2') to the 3.0 code - the migration check first (it writes nothing), the DSL fixes it asks for, then, on "move" and the user's yes, the move and its failures matched to their causes. Arguments - "check" (default) or "move". ([xsko/elasticore-agent-kit](https://github.com/xsko/elasticore-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/opencode/commands/elasticore-migrate.md) · ★2)
- **elasticore-review**: Review a change in an ElastiCORE project with the elasticore-reviewer agent - hand edits to generated files, DSL checked against the project's guides and the generator the build runs, and the business impact. Arguments - a git range or branch (default - the changes not yet committed), and "full" to also build. ([xsko/elasticore-agent-kit](https://github.com/xsko/elasticore-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/opencode/commands/elasticore-review.md) · ★2)
- **elasticsearch-best-practices**: Elasticsearch development best practices for indexing, querying, and search optimization ([ziyabicilecommerce-hub/ai-cash-machine](https://github.com/ziyabicilecommerce-hub/ai-cash-machine/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/elasticsearch-best-practices.md))
- **elasticsearch-patterns**: Designs Elasticsearch mappings, analyzers, and search queries with relevance tuning discipline. Excludes log pipeline administration. ([xcell321/reviewer-harness](https://github.com/xcell321/reviewer-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/elasticsearch-patterns.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **elderly**: [subject] elderly person, weathered expressive features ([geevarghesekthomas84-sys/image-style-skill](https://github.com/geevarghesekthomas84-sys/image-style-skill/blob/HEAD/dist/marketplace/image-style/commands/elderly.md) · ★4)
